Same model family as the architect — use the grok lane when you want vendor diversity. - **opus-implementer-high**: Opus implementation agent at high effort — the quota-friendly tier for S-sized, well-specified, mechanical parity packages (copy ports, option lists, small advisory branches, UI stubs). Same conventions as opus-implementer, leaner reasoning budget. ([Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes](https://github.com/Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-implementer-high.md))
- **opus-implementer-low**: Implementation lane running Claude Opus at LOW reasoning effort. Route mechanical or boilerplate changes here where the spec fully determines the output — but prefer the grok lane for this class of work unless you specifically want a Claude producer. Receives the standard five-part spec, implements the change itself (no external CLI), runs the verification, and returns a structured report with evidence. ([huytdps13400/fable-advisor](https://github.com/huytdps13400/fable-advisor/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-implementer-low.md) · ★1)
- **opus-implementer-max**: Opus implementation agent at MAX effort — for the subtler parity work packages assigned to Opus (nuanced UX ports, tricky state interactions, packages whose failure modes reviews catch late). Same conventions as opus-implementer, deeper reasoning budget. ([Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes](https://github.com/Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-implementer-max.md))
- **opus-implementer-medium**: Implementation lane running Claude Opus at MEDIUM reasoning effort. Route non-trivial logic that isn't safety-critical here — standard feature code, moderate refactors. Receives the standard five-part spec, implements the change itself (no external CLI), runs the verification, and returns a structured report with evidence. Same model family as the architect — use the grok lane when you want vendor diversity. ([huytdps13400/fable-advisor](https://github.com/huytdps13400/fable-advisor/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-implementer-medium.md) · ★1)
- **opus-implementer-xhigh**: Heavy implementation delegate (Opus, xhigh effort). Same contract as opus-implementer but for intrinsically hard code: concurrency and async correctness, subtle multi-site refactors, perf-critical paths, security-sensitive surfaces, tricky algorithms. ([LivinTribunal/architect-skill](https://github.com/LivinTribunal/architect-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-implementer-xhigh.md) · 2 copies)
- **opus-implementor**: Pane agent (herdr panes `opus-imple-&lt;n&gt;`, `claude --agent opus-implementor`): Opus implementer of one briefed Mado work package, test-first. Not for audits, plans, PRs, merges or device records. ([anzchy/mado](https://github.com/anzchy/mado/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-implementor.md) · ★1)
- **opus-janitor**: Council janitor. Runs last on captain's draft. Flags bloat, returns BLOAT_FLAG + CUTS list + WORD_COUNT. Never trims itself — captain removes. Runs on Opus 5 as a live teammate. ([ANcpLua/ancplua-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ANcpLua/ancplua-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/council/agents/opus-janitor.md) · ★6)
- **opus-judge**: Judgement lane — a frontier model, for planning and final verdict/synthesis only. Opus 5.5 at max effort. Use in short bursts, two touches per feature max — a plan up front, or a verdict / cross-attempt synthesis / ship go-no-go at the end. Thinking, not typing — never a resident worker, never code-writing. ([reasance/delegation-kit](https://github.com/reasance/delegation-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-judge.md))
- **opus-low**: Internal Kerd routing agent for Opus at low reasoning effort. Invoke only when Kerd Conductor or Agent explicitly selects kerd:opus-low for a delegated job; do not choose it for ordinary requests. The name carries the model and the effort so the running-job list shows both. The dispatching call still names the model (opus); this definition sets the same model, so a call that omits it still resolves to Opus, unless the host forces or restricts the subagent model. ([anthonymaley/Kerd](https://github.com/anthonymaley/Kerd/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-low.md) · ★3)
- **opus-low**: Opus implementation agent at low reasoning effort, for session-dispatched build tasks whose contract is already settled elsewhere. ([dwcoates/doom-emacs-config](https://github.com/dwcoates/doom-emacs-config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-low.md) · ★3)
- **opus-low**: Delegated worker (Opus) at low effort, for a quick expert look at a small, fully supplied context where depth is not needed. Never for mechanical steps, which go to sonnet-low. ([bantoilatoi/AgentSkills](https://github.com/bantoilatoi/AgentSkills/blob/HEAD/setup/claude/agents/opus-low.md))
- **opus-low**: Router worker: Opus 5.5 at low effort. Started only by the router. ([Light505/gswitchtool](https://github.com/Light505/gswitchtool/blob/HEAD/integrations/router-plugin/agents/opus-low.md))
- **opus-low**: Fixed-tier executor (claude-opus-5-5, low effort). Use per the global routing matrix — never inherits the orchestrator's model. ([rwspatin/agent-orchestration](https://github.com/rwspatin/agent-orchestration/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-low.md))
- **opus-low**: General-purpose worker running Opus at low reasoning effort. Use for well-specified, routine subtasks — mechanical edits, fan-out searches, summarization, boilerplate, log/output triage — where Opus-level competence is wanted without deep-reasoning latency or token cost. Not for architecture decisions, ambiguous specs, or subtle debugging. ([Sartonio/ai-first-starter](https://github.com/Sartonio/ai-first-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-low.md))
- **opus-max**: Internal Kerd routing agent for Opus at max reasoning effort. Invoke only when Kerd Conductor or Agent explicitly selects kerd:opus-max for a delegated job; do not choose it for ordinary requests. The name carries the model and the effort so the running-job list shows both. The dispatching call still names the model (opus); this definition sets the same model, so a call that omits it still resolves to Opus, unless the host forces or restricts the subagent model. ([anthonymaley/Kerd](https://github.com/anthonymaley/Kerd/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-max.md) · ★3)
- **opus-max**: Fallback seat, Opus at effort max: takes the work of the Fable seat that names it, only when Fable is exhausted or unavailable. Never picked first. ([fo0/listen-to-me](https://github.com/fo0/listen-to-me/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-max.md) · ★3 · 4 copies)
- **opus-max**: Opus at effort max, for exceptions only, with a one-line reason in the assignment: an xhigh assignment that failed repeatedly, or a change that is especially critical. ([fo0/tubetrend](https://github.com/fo0/tubetrend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-max.md) · ★2)
- **opus-max**: Generic worker pinned to model opus at effort max. Use only when explicitly dispatching per the superpowers-dispatch policy — never auto-select. ([UmutEmreOnder/superpowers-dispatch](https://github.com/UmutEmreOnder/superpowers-dispatch/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-max.md) · ★1)
- **opus-max**: Claude Opus at MAX reasoning effort. Reserve for genuinely hard single tasks - make-or-break adversarial verification, hardest design judgments, final gates on high-stakes changes. Costs materially more than xhigh; default to opus-xhigh unless the task earns max. ([Chasonnnn/agentic-engineering-skills-personal](https://github.com/Chasonnnn/agentic-engineering-skills-personal/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-max.md))
- **opus-max**: Router worker: Opus 5.5 at max effort. Started only by the router. ([Light505/gswitchtool](https://github.com/Light505/gswitchtool/blob/HEAD/integrations/router-plugin/agents/opus-max.md))
- **opus-medium**: Default builder for delegated work (Opus at medium effort). Use for features, whole products from a spec, multi-file changes, refactors, tests, research, docs, fix rounds from a review, bugs whose cause is known, and ordinary code reviews. ([ychampion/ystack](https://github.com/ychampion/ystack/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/opus-medium.md) · ★5)
- **opus-medium**: Internal Kerd routing agent for Opus at medium reasoning effort. Invoke only when Kerd Conductor or Agent explicitly selects kerd:opus-medium for a delegated job; do not choose it for ordinary requests. The name carries the model and the effort so the running-job list shows both. The dispatching call still names the model (opus); this definition sets the same model, so a call that omits it still resolves to Opus, unless the host forces or restricts the subagent model. ([anthonymaley/Kerd](https://github.com/anthonymaley/Kerd/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-medium.md) · ★3)
- **opus-medium**: Opus implementation agent at medium reasoning effort, for session-dispatched build tasks in this repo. ([dwcoates/doom-emacs-config](https://github.com/dwcoates/doom-emacs-config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-medium.md) · ★3)
- **opus-medium**: Opus at effort medium, the one seat below the floor: watches a pipeline or collects raw data — follows a CI or deploy run, or runs the commands its assignment names and stores their output where it says, and reports the paths and the result. No merge, no change, no decision. ([fo0/listen-to-me](https://github.com/fo0/listen-to-me/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-medium.md) · ★3 · 4 copies)
- **opus-medium**: Claude row of the agx roster (`agx worker roster --json`): Opus at medium effort. For an ambiguous spec, design judgment, factual accuracy, or a multi-repo change. Pick by the roster table; no justification line is required. ([fuyutarow/dotfiles](https://github.com/fuyutarow/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agents/claude/agents/opus-medium.md) · ★3)
- **opus-medium**: Opus at effort medium, the one seat below high: watches a pipeline — follows a CI or deploy run and reports its result. No merge, no change, no decision. ([fo0/tubetrend](https://github.com/fo0/tubetrend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-medium.md) · ★2)
- **opus-medium**: Nadir worker on Opus at medium effort. Use for hard but bounded work that does not need your full thinking budget: a design-sensitive change with a settled direction. Give it a complete brief: files, acceptance criteria, constraints. ([NadirRouter/nadir-route-plugin](https://github.com/NadirRouter/nadir-route-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-medium.md) · ★2)
- **opus-medium**: Generic worker pinned to model opus at effort medium. Use only when explicitly dispatching per the superpowers-dispatch policy — never auto-select. ([UmutEmreOnder/superpowers-dispatch](https://github.com/UmutEmreOnder/superpowers-dispatch/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-medium.md) · ★1)
- **opus-medium**: Delegated worker (Opus) at medium effort, for short judgment with little output: reviewing a plan, choosing between approaches, adjudicating a disputed finding. ([bantoilatoi/AgentSkills](https://github.com/bantoilatoi/AgentSkills/blob/HEAD/setup/claude/agents/opus-medium.md))
- **opus-medium**: General-purpose agent pinned to opus-5 at medium reasoning effort. No role framing. ([Coding-downunder/ai-simply](https://github.com/Coding-downunder/ai-simply/blob/HEAD/operating-system/agents/opus-medium.md))
- **opus-medium**: Opus 5.5 at medium effort. The default for delegated work, and the floor for any task that reads code to reach a conclusion (an investigation, a diagnosis, a review, a design choice). ([gborges0727/claude-plugins](https://github.com/gborges0727/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/gborges-standard/agents/opus-medium.md))
- **opus-medium**: Router worker: Opus 5.5 at medium effort. Started only by the router. ([Light505/gswitchtool](https://github.com/Light505/gswitchtool/blob/HEAD/integrations/router-plugin/agents/opus-medium.md))
- **opus-medium**: Fixed-tier executor (claude-opus-5-5, medium effort). Use per the global routing matrix — never inherits the orchestrator's model. ([rwspatin/agent-orchestration](https://github.com/rwspatin/agent-orchestration/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-medium.md))
- **opus-meta-reviewer**: Pipeline evolution agent. Triggered every N runs by the orchestrator to evaluate whether the pipeline structure, agent configuration, or score weights need structural changes. Produces PipelineEvolution proposals via sdd_propose_evolution. Do NOT use for per-feature review — that is handled by the /code-review plugin. ([rubenzarroca/sdd-autopilot](https://github.com/rubenzarroca/sdd-autopilot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/opus-meta-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **opus-oracle**: Final escalation tier and judge on Claude Opus 5.5 — second opinions, decision critique, drift detection, and problems that defeated the reviewer ensemble (read-only) ([potentialdiffer/pi-agent-stuff](https://github.com/potentialdiffer/pi-agent-stuff/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-oracle.md))
- **opus-orchestrator**: Orchestrates complex multi-step tasks, delegates subtasks to cheaper tiers ([SonyHarv/claude-polyrouter](https://github.com/SonyHarv/claude-polyrouter/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-orchestrator.md) · ★2)
- **opus-orchestrator**: Opus main orchestrator. Thinks, decides, delegates context-heavy work to fixed-model satellites; keeps its own context lean. ([JaeCheon8587/Claudecode-For-Me](https://github.com/JaeCheon8587/Claudecode-For-Me/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-orchestrator.md) · ★1)
- **opus-owner**: Opus owner for a large or risky task the main loop has scoped — multi-file implementation, a repo sweep, a root-cause hunt, or a review that must read a lot. May delegate lookups to haiku-scout and mid-size slices to sonnet-implementer. Lean tool set: no skills, no MCP, no Artifact, so it costs a third of a general-purpose spawn. ([ucsandman/claude-harness](https://github.com/ucsandman/claude-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-owner.md) · ★32 · 2 copies)
- **opus-payload**: Subagent on Claude Opus 4.8 for authorized red-team work that reads attack payload files (malicious.py, malicious websites, injected records) when the astra subscription window is exhausted. Sami, 2026-09-30: no paid OpenAI API for these lanes; use Opus 4.8, 4.7 or 4.6. Full tool access; finishes the task it is handed and reports with evidence. ([sjawhar/dotfiles](https://github.com/sjawhar/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/omp/agents/opus-payload.md) · ★5)
- **opus-plan-coordinator**: Turns a locked design into an implementation plan and a set of executable Sonnet work packages. Use in the PLAN_DRAFT phase. ([Hbz00/hyperpowers](https://github.com/Hbz00/hyperpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-plan-coordinator.md) · ★3)
- **opus-plan-reviewer**: Reviews implementation plans (fallback when external LLMs unavailable) ([piercelamb/deep-plan](https://github.com/piercelamb/deep-plan/blob/HEAD/agents/opus-plan-reviewer.md) · ★103 · 5 copies)
- **opus-plan-reviewer**: Reviews implementation plans for footguns, security issues, performance problems, and architecture concerns. Runs first in /shipwright-plan Step 5, before external LLM review, and carries the gate when external review is unavailable. ([svenroth-ai/shipwright](https://github.com/svenroth-ai/shipwright/blob/HEAD/plugins/shipwright-plan/agents/opus-plan-reviewer.md) · ★14)
